\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Superclass for all sources, filters, and sinks in VTK. 

\doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm} is the superclass for all sources, filters, and sinks in VTK. It defines a generalized interface for executing data processing algorithms. Pipeline connections are associated with input and output ports that are independent of the type of data passing through the connections.

Instances may be used independently or within pipelines with a variety of architectures and update mechanisms. Pipelines are controlled by instances of \doxylink{classvtk_executive}{vtk\+Executive}. Every \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm} instance has an associated \doxylink{classvtk_executive}{vtk\+Executive} when it is used in a pipeline. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} executive is responsible for data flow. 

\label{doc-enum-members}

\Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_ac2bf3c779aa60908da5bc3df9b066db6}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!DesiredOutputPrecision@{DesiredOutputPrecision}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{DesiredOutputPrecision}{DesiredOutputPrecision}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_ac2bf3c779aa60908da5bc3df9b066db6} 
enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_algorithm_ac2bf3c779aa60908da5bc3df9b066db6}{vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Desired\+Output\+Precision}}}

Values used for setting the desired output precision for various algorithms. Currently, the following algorithms support changing their output precision:\+ \doxylink{classvtk_append_poly_data}{vtk\+Append\+Poly\+Data}, \doxylink{classvtk_clean_poly_data}{vtk\+Clean\+Poly\+Data}, \doxylink{classvtk_clip_poly_data}{vtk\+Clip\+Poly\+Data}, \doxylink{classvtk_connectivity_filter}{vtk\+Connectivity\+Filter}, \doxylink{classvtk_decimate_polyline_filter}{vtk\+Decimate\+Polyline\+Filter}, \doxylink{classvtk_decimate_pro}{vtk\+Decimate\+Pro}, \doxylink{classvtk_delaunay3_d}{vtk\+Delaunay3D}, \doxylink{classvtk_feature_edges}{vtk\+Feature\+Edges}, \doxylink{classvtk_glyph3_d}{vtk\+Glyph3D}, \doxylink{classvtk_hedge_hog}{vtk\+Hedge\+Hog}, \doxylink{classvtk_mask_points}{vtk\+Mask\+Points}, \doxylink{classvtk_poly_data_connectivity_filter}{vtk\+Poly\+Data\+Connectivity\+Filter}, \doxylink{classvtk_smooth_poly_data_filter}{vtk\+Smooth\+Poly\+Data\+Filter}, \doxylink{classvtk_static_clean_poly_data}{vtk\+Static\+Clean\+Poly\+Data}, \doxylink{classvtk_threshold_points}{vtk\+Threshold\+Points}, \doxylink{classvtk_tube_filter}{vtk\+Tube\+Filter}, \doxylink{classvtk_append_points}{vtk\+Append\+Points}, \doxylink{classvtk_transform_filter}{vtk\+Transform\+Filter}, virtual void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Add\+Input\+Connection (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{port}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_algorithm_output}{vtk\+Algorithm\+Output}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{input}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Add a connection to the given input port index. See \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_aac26b4138f0e8c07e45c74571f2c51a3}{Set\+Input\+Connection()} for details on input connections. This method is the complement to \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_a0d3aa5ee8ea54ee3c26beba2eb45e158}{Remove\+Input\+Connection()} in that it adds only the connection specified without affecting other connections. Typical usage is

filter2-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}Add\+Input\+Connection(0, filter1-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}Get\+Output\+Port(0)). \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a7478e51c4c99b096426e0c710f8f3448}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!AddInputDataObject@{AddInputDataObject}}

These functions return the update extent for output ports that use piece extents. Where port is not specified, it is assumed to be 0. \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a6e9fb321b2136bb58929ee3cfdbe84a4}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!HasExecutive@{HasExecutive}}
\index{HasExecutive@{HasExecutive}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HasExecutive()}{HasExecutive()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a6e9fb321b2136bb58929ee3cfdbe84a4} 
vtk\+Type\+Bool vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Has\+Executive (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Check whether this algorithm has an assigned executive. This will NOT create a default executive. \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a46fd9e0fd66e763aab2be5ded6e38de6}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!ModifyRequest@{ModifyRequest}}
\index{ModifyRequest@{ModifyRequest}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ModifyRequest()}{ModifyRequest()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a46fd9e0fd66e763aab2be5ded6e38de6} 
virtual int vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Modify\+Request (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information}{vtk\+Information}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{request}{, }\item[{int}]{when}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

This method gives the algorithm a chance to modify the contents of a request before or after (specified in the when argument) it is forwarded. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} default implementation is empty. Returns 1 on success, 0 on failure. When can be either vtk\+Executive::\+\+Before\+Forward or vtk\+Executive::\+\+After\+Forward. \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_aeb95198ad06b1b69c38b4fabd6d224dd}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!PrintSelf@{PrintSelf}}
\index{PrintSelf@{PrintSelf}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_aeb95198ad06b1b69c38b4fabd6d224dd} 
void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Print\+Self (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{ostream \&}]{os}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_indent}{vtk\+Indent}}}]{indent}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [override]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Methods invoked by print to print information about the object including superclasses. Typically not called by the user (use \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_aedadf6378465fd81c2592ef64262e2a5}{Print()} instead) but used in the hierarchical print process to combine the output of several classes. 

Reimplemented from \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_object_base_a08b8a2d0047a4d9b787c4a822bcda456}{vtk\+Object\+Base}}.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_ac17af57dc430cc844705ecc18bf5786b} 
vtk\+Type\+Bool vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Process\+Request (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information}{vtk\+Information}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{request}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_collection}{vtk\+Collection}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{in\+Info}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information_vector}{vtk\+Information\+Vector}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{out\+Info}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Version of \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_a4562e3c326792eff6ddac398c170b2a5}{Process\+Request()} that is wrapped. This converts the collection to an array and calls the other version. \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a4562e3c326792eff6ddac398c170b2a5}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!ProcessRequest@{ProcessRequest}}
\index{ProcessRequest@{ProcessRequest}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ProcessRequest()}{ProcessRequest()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a4562e3c326792eff6ddac398c170b2a5} 
virtual vtk\+Type\+Bool vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Process\+Request (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information}{vtk\+Information}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{request}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information_vector}{vtk\+Information\+Vector}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{in\+Info}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information_vector}{vtk\+Information\+Vector}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{out\+Info}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Upstream/\+\+Downstream requests form the generalized interface through which executives invoke a algorithm\textquotesingle{}s functionality. Upstream requests correspond to information flow from the algorithm\textquotesingle{}s outputs to its inputs. Downstream requests correspond to information flow from the algorithm\textquotesingle{}s inputs to its outputs.

A downstream request is defined by the contents of the request information object. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} input to the request is stored in the input information vector passed to Process\+Request. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} results of an downstream request are stored in the output information vector passed to Process\+Request.

An upstream request is defined by the contents of the request information object. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} input to the request is stored in the output information vector passed to Process\+Request. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} results of an upstream request are stored in the input information vector passed to Process\+Request.

It returns the boolean status of the pipeline (false means failure).

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a0d3aa5ee8ea54ee3c26beba2eb45e158} 
virtual void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Remove\+Input\+Connection (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{port}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_algorithm_output}{vtk\+Algorithm\+Output}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{input}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Remove a connection from the given input port index. See \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_aac26b4138f0e8c07e45c74571f2c51a3}{Set\+Input\+Connection()} for details on input connection. This method is the complement to \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_afea437e12d98e7745874708990d1dee0}{Add\+Input\+Connection()} in that it removes only the connection specified without affecting other connections. Typical usage is

filter2-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}Remove\+Input\+Connection(0, filter1-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}Get\+Output\+Port(0)).

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a42a55ca2c277aecc909ad592d12978aa} 
virtual void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Set\+Input\+Array\+To\+Process (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{idx}{, }\item[{int}]{port}{, }\item[{int}]{connection}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{field\+Association}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{attribute\+Typeor\+Name}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Set the input data arrays that this algorithm will process. String based version of \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_a02192f2f21b0e217a8be1314bac8052a}{Set\+Input\+Array\+To\+Process()}. Because field\+Association and field\+Attribute\+Type are enums, they cannot be easily accessed from scripting language. These methods provides an easy and safe way of passing association and attribute type information.

Field association is one of the following:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item vtk\+Data\+Object::\+\+FIELD\+\_\+\+ASSOCIATION\+\_\+\+POINTS
\item vtk\+Data\+Object::\+\+FIELD\+\_\+\+ASSOCIATION\+\_\+\+CELLS
\item vtk\+Data\+Object::\+\+FIELD\+\_\+\+ASSOCIATION\+\_\+\+NONE
\item vtk\+Data\+Object::\+\+FIELD\+\_\+\+ASSOCIATION\+\_\+\+POINTS\+\_\+\+THEN\+\_\+\+CELLS
\end{DoxyItemize}

Attribute type is one of the following:\+
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item vtk\+Data\+Set\+Attributes::\+\+SCALARS
\item vtk\+Data\+Set\+Attributes::\+\+VECTORS
\item vtk\+Data\+Set\+Attributes::\+\+NORMALS
\item vtk\+Data\+Set\+Attributes::\+\+TCOORDS
\item vtk\+Data\+Set\+Attributes::\+\+TENSORS
\end{DoxyItemize}

If the last argument is not an attribute type, it is assumed to be an array name. \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
void \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_adb34f48696ca20479b709e5e81b3932a}{Set\+Input\+Array\+To\+Process(int, int, int, int, int)} 
\end{DoxySeeAlso}


Reimplemented in \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_composite_poly_data_mapper_a42a55ca2c277aecc909ad592d12978aa}{vtk\+Composite\+Poly\+Data\+Mapper}}.

Array is expected to be in the input data object specified by (port, connection), and is stored under the given field\+Association with given name Internally, this algorithm references it at idx position.

\Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_aac26b4138f0e8c07e45c74571f2c51a3}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!SetInputConnection@{SetInputConnection}}
\index{SetInputConnection@{SetInputConnection}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{SetInputConnection()}{SetInputConnection()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_aac26b4138f0e8c07e45c74571f2c51a3} 
virtual void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Set\+Input\+Connection (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{port}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_algorithm_output}{vtk\+Algorithm\+Output}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{input}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Set the connection for the given input port index. Each input port of a filter has a specific purpose. A port may have zero or more connections and the required number is specified by each filter. Setting the connection with this method removes all other connections from the port. To add more than one connection use \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_afea437e12d98e7745874708990d1dee0}{Add\+Input\+Connection()}.

\doxylink{requirements_The}{The} input for the connection is the output port of another filter, which is obtained with \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_ae9008c5acfe2dfb68a03afb3852685e7}{Get\+Output\+Port()}. Typical usage is

filter2-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}Set\+Input\+Connection(0, filter1-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}Get\+Output\+Port(0)). 

Reimplemented in \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_abstract_hyper_tree_grid_mapper_ac1852c11b04a9c9c5df9bc6212550535}{vtk\+Abstract\+Hyper\+Tree\+Grid\+Mapper}}, \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_g_p_u_volume_ray_cast_mapper_ae0b9f3b417434c8391a184d11a2dbd81}{vtk\+GPUVolume\+Ray\+Cast\+Mapper}}, and \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_image_mathematics_a16cc858e9a90e54dbfb10ae504c78a1e}{vtk\+Image\+Mathematics}}.

\Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_ac6e988187e28a4b9283063436f575cbe}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!SetInputDataObject@{SetInputDataObject}}
\index{SetInputDataObject@{SetInputDataObject}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{SetInputDataObject()}{SetInputDataObject()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_ac6e988187e28a4b9283063436f575cbe} 
virtual void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Set\+Input\+Data\+Object (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{port}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_data_object}{vtk\+Data\+Object}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{data}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Sets the data-\/object as an input on the given port index. Setting the input with this method removes all other connections from the port. Internally, this method creates a \doxylink{classvtk_trivial_producer}{vtk\+Trivial\+Producer} instance and sets that as the input-\/connection for the given port. It is safe to call this method repeatedly with the same input data object. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} MTime of the \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm} will not change unless the data object changed. 

Reimplemented in \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_hyper_tree_grid_mapper_a1551f83cf2ea407d190192e710e521ca}{vtk\+Hyper\+Tree\+Grid\+Mapper}}.

\Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_aa2a5a19f344970c01c08661086f28a6e}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!SetProgressObserver@{SetProgressObserver}}
\index{SetProgressObserver@{SetProgressObserver}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{SetProgressObserver()}{SetProgressObserver()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_aa2a5a19f344970c01c08661086f28a6e} 
void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Set\+Progress\+Observer (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_progress_observer}{vtk\+Progress\+Observer}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

If an Progress\+Observer is set, the algorithm will report progress through it rather than directly. This means that it will call \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_a01d3abfac738c7ccf01548ae6833d8d3}{Update\+Progress()} on the Progress\+Observer rather than itself report it and set progress. This is most useful in situations where multiple threads are executing an algorithm at the same time and want to handle progress locally. \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a56142d639205c66db4e83fcaba3e58e7}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!SetProgressShiftScale@{SetProgressShiftScale}}
\index{SetProgressShiftScale@{SetProgressShiftScale}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{SetProgressShiftScale()}{SetProgressShiftScale()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a56142d639205c66db4e83fcaba3e58e7} 
void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Set\+Progress\+Shift\+Scale (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{double}]{shift}{, }\item[{double}]{scale}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Specify the shift and scale values to use to apply to the progress amount when {\ttfamily \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_a01d3abfac738c7ccf01548ae6833d8d3}{Update\+Progress}} is called. By default shift is set to 0, and scale is set to 1.\+0. This is useful when the \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm} instance is used as an internal algorithm to solve only a part of a whole problem.

If calling on a internal \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm}, make sure you take into consideration that values set of the outer \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm} as well since the outer \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm}{vtk\+Algorithm} itself may be nested in another algorithm.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Set\+Progress\+Shift\+Scale does not modify the MTime of the algorithm. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_ab8320d0e72262c08d5191563033dcc13}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!SetProgressText@{SetProgressText}}

void vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Set\+Progress\+Text (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ptext}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Set the current text message associated with the progress state. This may be used by a calling process/\+\+GUI. Note:\+ Because \doxylink{classvtk_algorithm_ab8320d0e72262c08d5191563033dcc13}{Set\+Progress\+Text()} is called from inside Request\+Data() it does not modify the algorithm object. Algorithms are not allowed to modify themselves from inside Request\+Data(). \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a66ed9aa274c6b144950444b56c9daeb0}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!SetReleaseDataFlag@{SetReleaseDataFlag}}

\Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a3159e3f06814b82f197af7849cd12ef4}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!Update@{Update}}
\index{Update@{Update}!vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Update()}{Update()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/3]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_algorithm_a3159e3f06814b82f197af7849cd12ef4} 
virtual vtk\+Type\+Bool vtk\+Algorithm::\+\+Update (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{port}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_information_vector}{vtk\+Information\+Vector}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{requests}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

This method enables the passing of data requests to the algorithm to be used during execution (in addition to bringing a particular port up-\/to-\/date). \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} requests argument should contain an information object for each port that requests need to be passed. For each of those, the pipeline will copy all keys to the output information before execution. This is equivalent to:\+ \begin{DoxyVerb}* algorithm->UpdateInformation();
* for (int i=0; i<algorithm->GetNumberOfOutputPorts(); i++)
* {
* vtkInformation* portRequests = requests->GetInformationObject(i);
* if (portRequests)
* {
* algorithm->GetOutputInformation(i)->Append(portRequests);
* }
* }
* algorithm->Update();
* \end{DoxyVerb}
 Available requests include UPDATE\+\_\+\+PIECE\+\_\+\+NUMBER(), UPDATE\+\_\+\+NUMBER\+\_\+\+OF\+\_\+\+PIECES() UPDATE\+\_\+\+EXTENT() etc etc. 

Reimplemented in \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_cell_grid_mapper_a4918d5a9a988c5f7dc8e51c59dba52c5}{vtk\+Cell\+Grid\+Mapper}}, \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_image_reslice_mapper_a8a17054e0c8de285924118d4c1a78b74}{vtk\+Image\+Reslice\+Mapper}}, and \doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_poly_data_mapper_a9e6913034e43af9e5a4cee639f4413db}{vtk\+Poly\+Data\+Mapper}}.

This detects when the Update\+Extent will generate no data This condition is satisfied when the Update\+Extent has zero volume (0,-\/1,...) or the Update\+Number\+Of\+Pieces is 0. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} source uses this call to determine whether to call Execute. \Hypertarget{classvtk_algorithm_a9c3db4e3575680f554791be35b8d50cd}\index{vtkAlgorithm@{vtkAlgorithm}!UpdateInformation@{UpdateInformation}}

vtk\+Algorithm::\+vtk\+Set\+Macro (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{Abort\+Output}]{}{, }\item[{bool}]{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Set/\+\+Get an internal variable used to communicate between the algorithm and executive. If the executive sees this value is set, it will initialize the output data and pass the ABORTED flag downstream.

Check\+Abort sets this value to true if the function returns true.
